    Launch HN: Magnitude (YC S25) – エージェント向けの自己最適化推論エンジン

    AI/ML 💬 28件 元コメントを読む ↗ 元記事を読む ↗ ▲ 52点

    AIエージェントの推論コストを削減する自己最適化エンジンが注目されている。マグニチュードはエージェントがタスクを繰り返す中で自ら推論を効率化し、長期的な運用コストを下げる。日本のスタートアップもAIエージェントの性能とコストバランスに注目が集まりつつある。

    以下は、Hacker News のコメントから作成した要約です。

    AIコメント要約（全文）

    以下は、Hacker News のコメントから作成した要約です。 **主な議論点** コミュニティの議論は、主に「Magnitude」が既存の推論エンジン（特にllama.cpp） compared でどの程度優れているか、およびその実用性の観点から进行了。具体的には、ローカル環境での性能最適化、メモリ（VRAM/RAM）管理、 large context size におけるパフォーマンス劣化、そして特定の硬件（AMD GPU、Strix Halo）への対応の有無が焦点となった。 **賛否両論** 賛成派は、エージェント用途における推論とツール呼び出しの統合や、温度制御などのランタイム設定の柔軟性を評価した。しかし、反対派は、llama.cppやOllama、llama-cpp-pythonなど、より成熟且つ最適化されたエンジンが既に存在することを指摘し、「llama.cppより速い」という目標は低基準であると批判した。特に、最適なSpeculative Decodingの使用、KVキャッシュのVRAM使用量、 large context における性能低下という3つの「失敗モード」が既存エンジンの課題として挙げられ、Magnitudeがこれらをどう解決するかが問われる。 **注目コメント** 特に洞察のあるコメントとして、以下の点が挙げられる。 1. **実務的なpain point**: 外部ポリシーによるスロットリング（温度制御）や、ツール呼び出しのオーバーヘッド（`ls`に数十秒かかるなど）が実際の作業のボトルネックになっているという具体的な 使用例の報告。 2. **自動最適化への期待**: ユーザーの硬件やコンテキストサイズに応じて最適なモデルバリアントを自動的に選択する機能への関心。これは、現在の手動設定の煩雑さを解消する重要なポイントである。 3. **開発者による自己最適化**: コメント執筆者が自身のllama.cppを自動更新・最適化するエージェントを構築しているという、Magnitudeのコンセプトと重なる高度な使用例の紹介。これは、自己最適化型エンジンの潜在能力を示す一例である。

    コミットメッセージを思考ツールとして使う

    Other 💬 21件 元コメントを読む ↗ 元記事を読む ↗ ▲ 57点

    コミットメッセージを「思考の道具」として使うアプローチは、コードだけでなく開発者の思考そのものを記録する試みだ。複雑な設計判断を言葉で整理することで、チーム内の知識共有が深まる。日本の開発文化でalso 'ドキュメント文化'が浸透れば、この方法も自然に受け入れられそうだ。

    「コミットメッセージをコードより先に書くと考えを整理できる」という点が議論の中心で、書くことは思考そのものだと指摘される。

    AIコメント要約（全文）

    「コミットメッセージをコードより先に書くと考えを整理できる」という点が議論の中心で、書くことは思考そのものだと指摘される。一方、説明をドキュメントやADRに置くべきか迷う声もあり、ファイルは普遍的で目に付きやすいが、Git歴史は編集不可かつ特定コミットに紐付く利点があるとされる。そこで注目されたのはgit‑notesの活用で、コミットに隣接しブランチ構造を保ちながら歴史を汚さず注釈を付けられる手法として評価された。さらに、異なるLLMファミリを使ってコミットをレビューしメッセージを生成し、不一致時に手動レビューを挟むワークフローも紹介され、自動支援と人間の判断のバランスが話題となった。

    ブルームバーグターミナルの歴史について

    Other 💬 46件 元コメントを読む ↗ 元記事を読む ↗ ▲ 132点

    ブルームバーグターミナルは金融データの専用端末として数十年進化を遂げた。今日のAIやクラウドが金融情報に革命を起こす中、その歴史は「専閲門」と「オープン」の対照を浮き彫りにする。日本の金融ITでも伝統と革新のバランスが問われている。

    ・主な議論点: ビルグンバーグ端末の情報密集的なUIデザインへの評価、HTTP以前の技術を継承しながら現代のChromiumベースに進化した点、バックワード互換性の重要性（1985年の端末でも現在のニュースが表示可能）、そして個人投資家向けの代替ツール不足が指摘された。

    AIコメント要約（全文）

    ・主な議論点: ビルグンバーグ端末の情報密集的なUIデザインへの評価、HTTP以前の技術を継承しながら現代のChromiumベースに進化した点、バックワード互換性の重要性（1985年の端末でも現在のニュースが表示可能）、そして個人投資家向けの代替ツール不足が指摘された。 ・賛否両論: 端末の濃密で効率的な情報表示に対する賛辞が多く、「必要な情報を最小限に」表示する設計哲学が現代の航空機のコックピットディスプレイに似ているとの比喩もなされた。一方で、代替ツールの存在不足や高価なサブスクリプションモデルに対する批判的な意見もあり、Redditのwallstreetbetsコミュニティでの「クラッキング」に関するコメントが場の雰囲気を覆す議論も見られた。 ・注目コメント: ビルグンバーグ社が1985年代の端末ハードウェアを現在のニュースフィードで稼働させているという話題に対し、「彼らのバックワード互換性への執着は技術的な選択ではなく信仰のようなものだ」との指摘があり、企業文化としての深さが浮き彫りになった。また、個人投資家が同等の情報密集型UIを安価に利用できない現状に対する「小規模ショップがこの隙間を埋めようとしているが、真のビルグンバーグ端末の代替とはなっていない」とのコメントも興味深い。

    You Said No MCP

    AI/ML 💬 286件 元コメントを読む ↗ 元記事を読む ↗ ▲ 507点

    MCP（Model Context Protocol）に対する懐疑論的見解がまとめられた記事。MCPはAIツール間のインターフェースを統一しようとするが、過度の抽象化は開発者体験を損なう可能性もある。日本のエンジニアにとって「標準化の落とし穴」として参考になる。

    主な議論点は、MCP（Model‑Context‑Protocol）が本当に有用かどうかという点で、一部は自然言語でアプリの設定や自動化が可能になり開発負荷が減ると評価し、macOSアプリやCrank、BetterTouchToolでの実装例を挙げた。

    AIコメント要約（全文）

    主な議論点は、MCP（Model‑Context‑Protocol）が本当に有用かどうかという点で、一部は自然言語でアプリの設定や自動化が可能になり開発負荷が減ると評価し、macOSアプリやCrank、BetterTouchToolでの実装例を挙げた。一方で、過去に「MCPは死んだ」と主張したインフルエンサーが多く、セキュリティや可観測性、チームワークフローへの適用を無視した過度なハイスペック主張が批判された。賛否は、MCPの現状の不完全さ（OpenAIのPromptサポート欠如や実装のばらつき）を許容するか、より最適な別手段を待つかで分かれている。注目コメントでは、MCPはUSB‑CやNVMeのように欠点があるが広く互換性がありエンドユーザーに使いやすいため、時間とともに改善されると評価し、現在の広がりが価値があると指摘した。

    バーニングマンの死亡率 – 統計学の短いレッスン

    Other 💬 41件 元コメントを読む ↗ 元記事を読む ↗ ▲ 57点

    バーニングマンの死亡率データを題材にした統計リテラシーの教訓。少数の事故を「メディアの都市伝説」と誤解すると、冷静なリスク分析が歪む。日本でも大規模イベントやカンファレンスの安全管理に、このようなデータ思考が求められている。

    主な議論点は、Burning Manにおける死亡率の統計が選択バイアスにより歪んでいるか、あるいは主催者による安全対策（衛生施設、銃・爆発物禁止、道路網、ゾーニング、レンジャー等）がリスクを実質的に低減しているかという点。

    AIコメント要約（全文）

    主な議論点は、Burning Manにおける死亡率の統計が選択バイアスにより歪んでいるか、あるいは主催者による安全対策（衛生施設、銃・爆発物禁止、道路網、ゾーニング、レンジャー等）がリスクを実質的に低減しているかという点。さらに、参加者は健康・余裕がある層に限られるため一般人口との比較は意味がないという指摘と、イベント外での死亡（搬送先での死や往復道路事故）がカウントされていないことへの批判も見られた。 賛否両論として、選択バイアスを死者数低減の主因だと見る意見に対し、主催者が事故や死亡を契機に段階的に文明的インフラを築き上げてきたという評価がある。また、環境志向を掲げつつ使い捨て文化や過度なルール、排他的な雰囲気を指摘する批判と、そうしたルールが安全を守る必要不可欠な仕組みだと擁護する声が分かれた。 注目コメントとして、「イベント境界外での死亡はカウントされず、搬送先での死や往復道路事故も含まれていない」という指摘が、統計の不備を突く洞察として挙げられた。

    私は17TBのマイクロソフトレコードにアクセスできるでしょう

    Security 💬 74件 元コメントを読む ↗ 元記事を読む ↗ ▲ 164点

    マイクロソフトの17TBレコードへのアクセス可能性を追いたたずねるセキュリティ記事。クラウド時代の「最小権限の原則」の重要性を改めて突きつける。日本の企業でもZero TrustやID管理の強化が急務となっている。

    主な議論点は、Microsoftが脆弱性開示記事に編集権を行使し、内容を削除・改変したことへの批判である。

    AIコメント要約（全文）

    主な議論点は、Microsoftが脆弱性開示記事に編集権を行使し、内容を削除・改変したことへの批判である。加えて、賞金5000ドルが他のMSRCバウンティと比べ極めて低く、報酬の妥当性が議論された。さらに、社内にJWT問題を防ぐMicrosoft Identity Service Essentials(MISE)があり、セキュア・フューチャー・イニシアティブで導入が進んでいたにもかかわらず、チームがコンプライアンス警告を意図的に遅らせたという指摘もあった。若手研究者が15歳で最初の報告を行い、今回も大きなインパクトを出したことに称賛が集まり、JWT仕様で「alg:none」が許可されたことへの疑問も見られた。

    TLA+ができることとできないこと

    Programming languages 💬 10件 元コメントを読む ↗ 元記事を読む ↗ ▲ 61点

    TLA+が形式検証で可能なことと限界を明快に整理。特に分散システムの設計前にモデル検査を行う価値が強調されている。日本の大規模システム開発でも「検証文化」の普及が求められている。

    主な議論点: TLA+は状態遷移の高レベル correctness 検証に強いが、弱メモリモデルやハードウェア側チャネル、浮動小数点の非結合性など低レベル詳細を直接扱えず、明示的なロジックや外部ツールが必要だという点。

    AIコメント要約（全文）

    主な議論点: TLA+は状態遷移の高レベル correctness 検証に強いが、弱メモリモデルやハードウェア側チャネル、浮動小数点の非結合性など低レベル詳細を直接扱えず、明示的なロジックや外部ツールが必要だという点。 賛否両論: weak memory モデリングについては、手動でキャッシュフラッシュ lógicaを追加するのは誤りやすいとの懸念がある一方で、Quint のような実行可能仕様言語や miri/loom のアナライザーが補完できるとの期待がある。また、形式手法だけで十分だという考えに対し、実際の理解やテストの必要性を強調する声もある。 注目コメント: Quint を紹介し、TLA+ のアクション temporal logic に基づく JavaScript 実行可能仕様としてツールが充実していると評価したコメント；さらに、1996 年の論文と「The Future of TLA+」から引用され、形式手法ではハードウェア側チャネルや浮動小数点非結合性を見つけられないことを指摘した洞察に富んだ意見。

    SDF vs. MSDF vs. スラグ: GPUテキストレンダリング

    Other 💬 36件 元コメントを読む ↗ 元記事を読む ↗ ▲ 94点

    GPUテキストレンダリング技術比較。SDFやMSDF、Slug各手法のトレードオフを解説し、パフォーマンスと品質のバランスを議論する。ゲームやVR/AR業界の日本開発者にも直結する技術トピックだ。

    **主な議論点** コミュニティでは、GPUおよびCPU向けのフォントラスタライザのアルゴリズム比較と最適化が中心に議論されました。

    AIコメント要約（全文）

    **主な議論点** コミュニティでは、GPUおよびCPU向けのフォントラスタライザのアルゴリズム比較と最適化が中心に議論されました。特に、Slug、SDF、MSDF、および新たなBezier曲線の分割技術（モノトニックセクションへの分割）についての性能と品質の違いが検討されました。また、距離場（distance field）に基づく手法とピクセル単位の判定手法のトレードオフ、およびヒンティングや中国語・日本語・韓国語などの大規模グリフ集合のメモリ問題に関する解決策も議論対象となりました。 **賛否両論** SDFやMSDFのような距離場技術は、シェーダーで簡単にアウトラインやエッジのソフト化などのエフェクトを追加できる点で評価されています。しかし、スケーリング時の角のシャープさや正確度については意見が分かれています。一方、Slugのような手法は高解像度ディスプレイや大きなサイズで優れた品質を提供する一方、エフェクトの追加やアンチエイリアシングの柔軟性には欠けるという批判もあります。 **注目コメント** ユーザーが提案したモノトニックセクションへのBezier曲線分割技術は、並列処理（GPUのワープ操作やSIMD）に最適化されており、パフォーマンスがSlugや従来の高速ラスタライザと競争できるとの主張が注目されました。さらに、SDFの補間による歪みをシェーダー内で補正可能である指摘や、動的アトラスを用いた大規模グリフのメモリ問題への解決策も興味深い見解として挙げられています。

    室内エネルギーハーベスティングと湿度管理のための moist-electric ワイヤレス

    Other 💬 25件 元コメントを読む ↗ 元記事を読む ↗ ▲ 36点

    湿度から電気を取り出すマイクロ波 Wallpapers の研究は、IoTデバイスの電源問題に新たな視点を与える。室内環境を「エネルギーソース」に変える発想は、日本の省電力社会にも応用可能だ。

    ・主な議論点: 久壁紙の概念に対する関心、長期耐久性やコスト、実用性についての疑問、湿度管理が電力生成より有用ではないかとの意見、IoTデバイス向けの応用可能性 ・賛否両論: 良い点としては、小規模IoTデバイスの電源供給や室内湿度管理の可能性が挙げられる。

    AIコメント要約（全文）

    ・主な議論点: 久壁紙の概念に対する関心、長期耐久性やコスト、実用性についての疑問、湿度管理が電力生成より有用ではないかとの意見、IoTデバイス向けの応用可能性 ・賛否両論: 良い点としては、小規模IoTデバイスの電源供給や室内湿度管理の可能性が挙げられる。懐疑的な意見では、耐久性の未検証、外観上の問題、コスト効率の低さが指摘される。また、大量導入時の全体的な湿度低下への懸念も報告された ・注目コメント: 38%から32%までの湿度低下は印象的だが、その部屋のサイズについての疑問や、「vaporware（実現不可能な商品）」とする批判的コメントもある。さらに、個人的な憧望として「自分の屋根で湿気から水を採取するマイクロデューディフェクター」のアイデアが紹介されている

    家族が最後に技術に取って変えられたとき

    Career/Work style 💬 176件 元コメントを読む ↗ 元記事を読む ↗ ▲ 82点

    家族を技術が「置き換えた」体験談。パーソナルAIやロボットが身近になる中、「人間的つながり」の価値を再考させる物語。日本のICT普及促進も、テクノロジーと人間関係の線量BALANCEが問われている。

    ・主な議論点 AI革命が産業革命以来最大の変化とされる中、実際の労働市場への影響やソフトウェア開発者の再スキル化可能性が議論された。

    SDF Public Access Unix System ... 設立1987年

    Systems/Infrastructure 💬 5件 元コメントを読む ↗ 元記事を読む ↗ ▲ 45点

    1987年創立のSDF Public Access Unix System は、インターネット黎明期から現在まで続く伝説的コミュニティ。オープンソース文化のルーツを体駱できるliving history*と言える。日本のハッカー文化にも多大な影響を与えてきた。

    主な議論点 SDFの利用実態と提供しているサービスへの関心が話題の中心だった。

    AIコメント要約（全文）

    AI/ML 💬 355件 元コメントを読む ↗ 元記事を読む ↗ ▲ 840点

    Opus 5.5 が nerfed されたかを検証する記事は、LLM ユーザーの間で実況される「アップデートの影響」に焦点を当てる。日本でもAIツールの性能変化について議論される場が増えつつある。

    主な議論点は、AnthropicのOpus 5.5が実際に「 nerf」（性能低下）されているかどうかで、Nerf Benchによる定量的追跡と、ユーザーの主観的感覚（「honeymoon効果」や複雑度の天井到達）が衝突していることです。

    AIコメント要約（全文）

    主な議論点は、AnthropicのOpus 5.5が実際に「 nerf」（性能低下）されているかどうかで、Nerf Benchによる定量的追跡と、ユーザーの主観的感覚（「honeymoon効果」や複雑度の天井到達）が衝突していることです。賛否両論として、一部のユーザーはフィードバックポップアップ後の即時劣化や個人的な体験から nerf を主張し、一方で他の参加者はベンチマークが顕著な変化を示さず、数千人のエンジニアによる日常的な微変更やキャッシュ調整が一時的な変動の原因だと指摘しています。注目コメントでは、「キャッシュやトークン最適化の調整が今日の最大の要因」と説明し、さらに「新モデルはリリース当初は余裕があるが、複雑度の上限にすぐ到達し、それが nerf と誤解される」というグラフィックを共有した意見が特に洞察に富んでいました。

技術的な側面で、特に「較正（calibration）」の問題が厳しく批判されています。具体には、ターゲット（Aprilタグ）を観測するセンサの配置が不適切で、測定したい変数の誤差が大きい、という根本的な設計の問題が指摘されています。これは、結果の信頼性そのものに疑問を投げかけています。 **注目コメント** * **技術的批判**: -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done -done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done-done
